import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  server: {
    port: 5173,
    // /api로 시작하는 요청을 스프링 부트(localhost:8080)로 전달
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: 'http://localhost:8080',
        changeOrigin: true,
        secure: false,
      },
    },
  },
  build: {
    // npm run build 시 스프링의 static 리소스 폴더로 바로 산출물 생성
    outDir: path.resolve(__dirname, '../../src/main/resources/static'),
    emptyOutDir: true,
  },
});

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import './App.css';
import { HomePage } from "./pages/HomePage.tsx";
import { SignupPage } from "./pages/SignupPage.tsx";
import { LoginPage } from "./pages/LoginPage.tsx";
import { KakaoCallback } from "./pages/KakaoCallback.tsx";
import { StayDetailPage } from "./pages/StayDetailPage.tsx";
import { ReservationPage } from "./pages/ReservationPage.tsx";
import { FindPasswordPage } from "./pages/FindPasswordPage.tsx";
import {WishlistPage} from "./pages/WishlistPage.tsx";
import { PaymentSuccessPage } from "./pages/PaymentSuccessPage.tsx";
import {ProfileEditPage} from "./pages/ProfileEditPage.tsx";
import {MyReservationsPage} from "./pages/MyReservationsPage.tsx";
import {HostReservationsPage} from "./pages/HostReservationsPage.tsx";
import {TourDetailPage} from "./pages/TourDetailPage.tsx";
import {TourWishlistPage} from "./pages/TourWishlistPage.tsx";
import {HostAccommodationPage} from "./pages/HostAccommodationPage.tsx";
import {AdminAccommodationsPage} from "./pages/AdminAccommodationsPage.tsx";


function App() {
    return (
        <BrowserRouter>
            <Routes>
                <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
                <Route path="/signup" element={<SignupPage />} />
                <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
                <Route path="/find-password" element={<FindPasswordPage />} />
                <Route path="/oauth/callback" element={<KakaoCallback />} />
                <Route path="/stays/:contentId" element={<StayDetailPage />} />
                <Route path="/reservation" element={<ReservationPage />} />
                <Route path="/wishlist" element={<WishlistPage />} />
                <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfileEditPage />} />
                <Route path="/payment/success" element={<PaymentSuccessPage />} />
                <Route path="/my-reservations" element={<MyReservationsPage />} />
                <Route path="/host/reservations" element={<HostReservationsPage />} />
                <Route path="/tours/:contentId" element={<TourDetailPage />} />
                <Route path="/tour-wishlist" element={<TourWishlistPage />} />
                <Route path="/host/accommodation" element={<HostAccommodationPage />} />
                <Route path="/admin/accommodations" element={<AdminAccommodationsPage />} />
            </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
    );
}

export default App;

/**
 * [테이블 5] ACCOMMODATION + JPA DTO 응답 규격
 */
export type  Stay= {
  contentId: string;       // PK, 식별자 (기존 id 대신 사용)
  memberId?: number | null;
  title: string;           // 숙소명 (기존 name 대신 사용)
  lodgingType?: string;    // AC01 ~ AC06 코드 또는 숙박 유형
  tel?: string;
  zipcode?: string;
  addr?: string;           // 기본 주소 (기존 address 대신 사용)
  checkinTime?: string;
  checkoutTime?: string;
  roomCount?: string;
  roomType?: string;
  parking?: string;
  subFacility?: string;
  overview?: string;       // 숙소 개요
  firstImage?: string;     // 대표 이미지 URL
  firstImage2?: string;    // 서브 썸네일 URL
  mapX?: string;
  mapY?: string;
  basePrice: number;       // 객실 최저가 (원화)
  remainingRooms?: number; // 잔여 객실 수
};

export type Room = {
  roomId: number;
  contentId: string;
  roomTitle?: string;
  roomSize?: string;
  roomBaseCount?: string;
  roomMaxCount?: string;
  pricePerNight?: number;
};

export type Booking = {
  contentId: string;
  roomId?: number;
  checkIn: string;
  checkOut: string;
  guests: number;
  guestName: string;
  phone: string;
};

export type BookingResponse = {
  reservationCode?: string;
  id?: number;
  status?: string;
  message?: string;
};

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';
import { KakaoStayMap } from './KakaoMap';

interface StayMapModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    stays: Stay[];
}

export function StayMapModal({ isOpen, onClose, stays }: StayMapModalProps) {
    // 1. 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px',
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1100px',
                    height: '85vh',
                    maxHeight: '780px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    padding: '28px',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} // 내부 클릭 시 모달 닫힘 방지
            >
                {/* 닫기 (X) 버튼 */}
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    aria-label="닫기"
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '24px',
                        right: '24px',
                        background: '#f1f5f9',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                        zIndex: 10,
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#e2e8f0')}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9')}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* 2. 기존 홈페이지 section-top 헤더 영역 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-end',
                        marginBottom: '18px',
                        paddingRight: '48px', // X 버튼과 겹치지 않도록 여백
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <span
                            className="eyebrow"
                            style={{
                                color: '#b45309',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                                display: 'block',
                                marginBottom: '4px',
                            }}
                        >
                            JEJU MAP GUIDE
                        </span>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            제주, 어디로 갈까요?
                        </h2>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            마커나 클러스터를 클릭하면 숙소 정보와 1박 요금을 확인하고 바로 이동할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                        style={{
                            color: '#0f766e',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                            padding: '6px 14px',
                            borderRadius: '20px',
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                        }}
                    >
                        실시간 등록 숙소 {stays.length}곳 탐색 가능
                    </span>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 지도 래퍼 카드 (모달 남은 높이를 꽉 채우도록 설정) */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        minHeight: 0,
                        borderRadius: '18px',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <KakaoStayMap stays={stays} level={9} height="100%" />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/KakaoMap.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoStayMapProps {
    stays: Stay[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail';
    zoomable?: boolean; // 💡 마우스 휠 확대/축소 허용 여부 (기본값: true)
}

export function KakaoStayMap({
                                 stays,
                                 level,
                                 height = '520px',
                                 mode = 'search',
                                 zoomable = true
                             }: KakaoStayMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null); // 💡 +/- 버튼 제어용 지도 인스턴스 보관
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 주입
    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const initKakaoMap = async () => {
            const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
            if (existingScript) {
                if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                    if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                } else {
                    existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => {
                        if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                    });
                }
                return;
            }

            let appKey = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_MAP_KEY || '';
            if (!appKey) {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/map-key');
                    if (res.ok) {
                        const data = await res.json();
                        appKey = data.mapKey;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('카카오 지도 키를 가져오지 못했습니다.', e);
                }
            }

            if (!appKey || !isMounted) return;

            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
            script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${appKey}&libraries=clusterer&autoload=false`;
            script.async = true;

            script.onload = () => {
                if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
            };

            document.head.appendChild(script);
        };

        initKakaoMap();

        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    // 2. 지도 초기화 및 마커 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validStays = stays.filter(
                (s) => s.mapX && s.mapY && !isNaN(Number(s.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(s.mapY))
            );

            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleStay = validStays.length === 1;

            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleStay) && validStays.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapX!)
                )
                : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            const zoomLevel = level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : isSingleStay ? 6 : 9);

            const options = {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            };
            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, options);
            mapInstanceRef.current = map;

            // 💡 마우스 휠 확대/축소 제어 (false일 때 스크롤 내려도 지도 축척이 변하지 않음)
            map.setZoomable(zoomable);

            // [케이스 A] 객실 상세페이지 전용 뷰 (mode === 'detail')
            if (isDetailPage && validStays.length > 0) {
                const stay = validStays[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                    position: relative;
                `;

                const uid = `${stay.contentId}-${zoomable ? 'popup' : 'inline'}`;

                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">
                            ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                        </span>
                        <button id="btn-close-detail-overlay-${uid}" style="
                            background: none;
                            border: none;
                            font-size: 14px;
                            color: #94a3b8;
                            cursor: pointer;
                            padding: 0 0 0 8px;
                            line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${stay.addr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-stay-addr-${uid}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 6px 0;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            주소 복사
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-bottom-${uid}" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px;
                            background-color: #ffffff;
                            color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                });

                overlay.setMap(map);

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-copy-stay-addr-${uid}`);
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-detail-overlay-${uid}`);
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-bottom-${uid}`);

                    if (copyBtn && stay.addr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(stay.addr as string);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // [케이스 B] 검색/홈페이지 지도 모드 (mode === 'search')
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;

            const markers = validStays.map((stay) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(stay.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(stay.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: position,
                });

                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 14px 16px;
                    width: 230px;
                    position: relative;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                content.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 8px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.addr || ''}
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #123f4d; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        ${money(stay.basePrice)} <span style="font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; color: #94a3b8;">/ 1박</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-detail-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 7px 0;
                            background-color: #123f4d;
                            color: #ffffff;
                            border: none;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.25,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    if (currentOverlay) {
                        currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-detail-${stay.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-${stay.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = () => navigate(`/stays/${stay.contentId}`);
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                if (isSingleStay) {
                    showOverlay();
                }

                return marker;
            });

            if (isSingleStay && markers.length > 0) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else if (markers.length > 0) {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, stays, level, mode, zoomable, navigate]);

    // 💡 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 버튼 핸들러 (휠 잠금 상태에서도 언제든 작동)
    const handleZoomIn = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() - 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    const handleZoomOut = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() + 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height }}>
            <div
                ref={mapContainerRef}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    height: '100%',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
                    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                }}
            >
                {!isLoaded && (
                    <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                        지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 💡 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 컨트롤 버튼 */}
            {isLoaded && (
                <div
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        right: '14px',
                        bottom: '14px',
                        zIndex: 10,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomIn}
                        title="지도 확대"
                        aria-label="지도 확대"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomOut}
                        title="지도 축소"
                        aria-label="지도 축소"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Minus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/KakaoTourMap.tsx

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { TourSummary } from '../api/tours';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoTourMapProps {
    tours: TourSummary[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail';
    zoomable?: boolean; // 💡 마우스 휠 확대/축소 허용 여부 (기본값: true)
    onNavigateDetail?: () => void;
}

export function KakaoTourMap({
                                 tours,
                                 level,
                                 height = '520px',
                                 mode = 'search',
                                 zoomable = true,
                                 onNavigateDetail
                             }: KakaoTourMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null); // 💡 +/- 버튼 제어용 지도 인스턴스 보관
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    const onNavigateDetailRef = useRef(onNavigateDetail);
    const navigateRef = useRef(navigate);

    useEffect(() => {
        onNavigateDetailRef.current = onNavigateDetail;
        navigateRef.current = navigate;
    }, [onNavigateDetail, navigate]);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 로드
    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const initKakaoMap = async () => {
            const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
            if (existingScript) {
                if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                    if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                } else {
                    existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => {
                        if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                    });
                }
                return;
            }

            let appKey = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_MAP_KEY || '';
            if (!appKey) {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/map-key');
                    if (res.ok) {
                        const data = await res.json();
                        appKey = data.mapKey;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('카카오 지도 키를 가져오지 못했습니다.', e);
                }
            }

            if (!appKey || !isMounted) return;

            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
            script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${appKey}&libraries=clusterer&autoload=false`;
            script.async = true;
            script.onload = () => {
                if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
            };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        };

        initKakaoMap();
        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    // 2. 마커 및 커스텀 오버레이 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validTours = tours.filter(
                (t) => t.mapX && t.mapY && !isNaN(Number(t.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(t.mapY))
            );

            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleTour = validTours.length === 1;

            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleTour) && validTours.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validTours[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validTours[0].mapX!)
                )
                : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            const zoomLevel = level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : isSingleTour ? 6 : 9);

            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            });
            mapInstanceRef.current = map;

            // 💡 마우스 휠 확대/축소 잠금 또는 허용
            map.setZoomable(zoomable);

            // [케이스 A] 관광지 상세페이지 하단 지도 모드
            if (isDetailPage && validTours.length > 0) {
                const tour = validTours[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const fullAddr = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();
                const uid = `${tour.contentId}-${zoomable ? 'popup' : 'inline'}`;

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">JEJU ATTRACTION</span>
                        <button id="btn-close-tour-detail-overlay-${uid}" style="
                            background: none; border: none; font-size: 14px; color: #94a3b8; cursor: pointer; padding: 0 0 0 8px; line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${tour.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${fullAddr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-tour-addr-${uid}" style="
                            flex: 1; padding: 6px 0; background-color: #f1f5f9; color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
                        ">주소 복사</button>
                        <button id="btn-close-tour-bottom-${uid}" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px; background-color: #ffffff; color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 11px; cursor: pointer;
                        ">닫기</button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                });

                overlay.setMap(map);

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-copy-tour-addr-${uid}`);
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-tour-detail-overlay-${uid}`);
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-tour-bottom-${uid}`);

                    if (copyBtn && fullAddr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullAddr);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // [케이스 B] 관광 지도 모달 모드 (전체 관광지 탐색)
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;

            const markers = validTours.map((tour) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(tour.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(tour.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({ position });

                const fullAddr = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();
                const thumbHtml = tour.firstImage
                    ? `<div style="width: 100%; height: 100px; border-radius: 8px; background-image: url('${tour.firstImage}'); background-size: cover; background-position: center; margin-bottom: 8px; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;"></div>`
                    : '';

                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    width: 240px;
                    position: relative;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                content.innerHTML = `
                    ${thumbHtml}
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        제주 관광명소
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${tour.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 10px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${fullAddr || '제주특별자치도'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-tour-detail-${tour.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1; padding: 7px 0; background-color: #123f4d; color: #ffffff;
                            border: none; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-tour-close-${tour.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px; background-color: #f1f5f9; color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.15,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    if (currentOverlay) currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-tour-detail-${tour.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-tour-close-${tour.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = () => {
                                if (onNavigateDetailRef.current) onNavigateDetailRef.current();
                                navigateRef.current(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`);
                            };
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                if (isSingleTour) showOverlay();
                return marker;
            });

            if (isSingleTour && markers.length > 0) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else if (markers.length > 0) {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, tours, level, mode, zoomable]);

    // 💡 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 버튼 핸들러
    const handleZoomIn = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() - 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    const handleZoomOut = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() + 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height }}>
            <div
                ref={mapContainerRef}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    height: '100%',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                }}
            >
                {!isLoaded && (
                    <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                        관광 지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 💡 우측 하단 확대(+) / 축소(-) 컨트롤 버튼 */}
            {isLoaded && (
                <div
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        right: '14px',
                        bottom: '14px',
                        zIndex: 10,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomIn}
                        title="지도 확대"
                        aria-label="지도 확대"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',

                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}

                        onClick={handleZoomOut}

import { ArrowRight, MapPin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';
import type { useReservation } from '../hooks/useReservation';

type Reservation = ReturnType<typeof useReservation>;

export function BookingModal({ reservation }: { reservation: Reservation }) {
  const { selected, close } = reservation;
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (!selected) return null;

  const bgImage = selected.firstImage || selected.firstImage2 || '/jeju-stays.png';

  const handleGoDetail = () => {
    close();
    navigate(`/stays/${selected.contentId}`);
  };

  return (
      <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) close(); }}>
        <section className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="booking-title" style={{ maxWidth: '480px' }}>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={close} aria-label="닫기">
            <X size={21} />
          </button>
          <div className="modal-image crop-0" style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${bgImage})`, height: '220px' }} />
          <div className="modal-body" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            <span className="eyebrow">JEJU STAY</span>
            <h2 id="booking-title" style={{ fontSize: '22px', margin: '4px 0 10px 0' }}>{selected.title}</h2>
            <p className="modal-address" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <MapPin size={16} />
              {selected.addr} · {getLodgingTypeName(selected.lodgingType)}
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#475569', lineHeight: 1.6, margin: '14px 0' }}>
              {selected.overview ? `${selected.overview.slice(0, 110)}...` : '아늑하고 편안한 제주 숙소입니다.'}
            </p>
            <div className="modal-price" style={{ margin: '16px 0 20px 0', padding: '12px 16px', background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '8px' }}>
              1박 최저가 <strong style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '18px', marginLeft: '6px' }}>{money(selected.basePrice)}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
                type="button"
                className="reserve-button"
                onClick={handleGoDetail}
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '14px',
                  backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  border: 'none',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '8px'
                }}
            >
              숙소 상세 및 객실 보러가기 <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
  );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/DateRangePicker.tsx

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Calendar, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

interface DateRangePickerProps {
    checkIn: string;   // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
    checkOut: string;  // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
    onChange: (checkIn: string, checkOut: string) => void;
}

export function DateRangePicker({ checkIn, checkOut, onChange }: DateRangePickerProps) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectingStart, setSelectingStart] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [hoverDate, setHoverDate] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // 달력 기준월 (체크인 날짜 기준 또는 오늘 기준)
    const [viewDate, setViewDate] = useState(() => {
        const d = checkIn ? new Date(checkIn) : new Date();
        return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1);
    });


    const pickerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 오늘 날짜 문자열
    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);

    // 외부 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            if (pickerRef.current && !pickerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
                setIsOpen(false);
                setSelectingStart(null);
            }
        };
        if (isOpen) document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }, [isOpen]);

    // 이전달 / 다음달 이동
    const handlePrevMonth = () => {
        setViewDate(new Date(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth() - 1, 1));
    };
    const handleNextMonth = () => {
        setViewDate(new Date(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth() + 1, 1));
    };

    // 날짜 클릭 핸들러
    const handleDateClick = (dateStr: string) => {
        if (!selectingStart) {
            // 첫 번째 클릭: 체크인 설정
            setSelectingStart(dateStr);
        } else {
            // 두 번째 클릭: 체크아웃 설정
            if (dateStr > selectingStart) {
                onChange(selectingStart, dateStr);
                setSelectingStart(null);
                setIsOpen(false);
            } else {
                // 이전 날짜를 클릭한 경우 다시 체크인으로 세팅
                setSelectingStart(dateStr);
            }
        }
    };

    // 박수 계산
    const nights = useMemo(() => {
        if (!checkIn || !checkOut) return 0;
        const s = new Date(checkIn);
        const e = new Date(checkOut);
        const diff = (e.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
        return diff > 0 ? diff : 0;
    }, [checkIn, checkOut]);

    // 특정 월의 날짜 그리드 생성 헬퍼
    const renderCalendarMonth = (year: number, month: number) => {
        const firstDayOfWeek = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay(); // 0(일) ~ 6(토)
        const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();

        const activeStart = selectingStart || checkIn;
        const activeEnd = selectingStart ? (hoverDate || selectingStart) : checkOut;

        const cells = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < firstDayOfWeek; i++) {
            cells.push(<div key={`empty-${i}`} style={{ height: '36px' }} />);
        }

        for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
            const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
            const isPast = dateStr < todayStr;
            const isStart = dateStr === activeStart;
            const isEnd = dateStr === activeEnd;
            const isInRange = activeStart && activeEnd && dateStr > activeStart && dateStr < activeEnd;

            cells.push(
                <button
                    key={dateStr}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isPast}
                    onClick={() => handleDateClick(dateStr)}
                    onMouseEnter={() => selectingStart && setHoverDate(dateStr)}
                    style={{
                        height: '36px',
                        width: '100%',
                        border: 'none',
                        background: isStart || isEnd ? '#123f4d' : isInRange ? '#e6f4f1' : 'transparent',
                        color: isStart || isEnd ? '#ffffff' : isPast ? '#cbd5e1' : '#1e293b',
                        fontWeight: isStart || isEnd ? 700 : 500,
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        cursor: isPast ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        borderRadius: isStart ? '8px 0 0 8px' : isEnd ? '0 8px 8px 0' : '0',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'background 0.15s ease'
                    }}
                >
                    {d}
                </button>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div style={{ width: '260px' }}>
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '15px', marginBottom: '14px', color: '#0f172a' }}>
                    {year}년 {month + 1}월
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: '2px', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                    <span>일</span><span>월</span><span>화</span><span>수</span><span>목</span><span>금</span><span>토</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: '2px 0' }}>
                    {cells}
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    };

    return (
        <div ref={pickerRef} style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%' }}>
            {/* 1. 트리거 인풋 버튼 */}
            <div
                onClick={() => setIsOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '10px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    userSelect: 'none'
                }}
            >
                <Calendar size={20} color="#49717a" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#819296', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        일정 {nights > 0 && `(${nights}박)`}
                    </span>
                    <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#17343d', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                        {checkIn ? checkIn.slice(5) : '체크인'} ~ {checkOut ? checkOut.slice(5) : '체크아웃'}
                    </strong>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 2. 팝업 모달창 (2개월 듀얼 뷰) */}
            {isOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: 'calc(100% + 14px)',
                        left: 0,
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        boxShadow: '0 12px 36px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '24px 28px',
                        zIndex: 1000,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        gap: '20px'
                    }}
                >
                    {/* 상단 월 이동 네비게이션 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#0f766e' }}>
                            {selectingStart ? '체크아웃 날짜를 선택해 주세요' : '체크인 날짜를 선택해 주세요'}
                        </span>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handlePrevMonth}
                                style={{ background: '#f1f5f9', border: 'none', borderRadius: '50%', width: '32px', height: '32px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                <ChevronLeft size={16} color="#475569" />
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleNextMonth}
                                style={{ background: '#f1f5f9', border: 'none', borderRadius: '50%', width: '32px', height: '32px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                            >
                                <ChevronRight size={16} color="#475569" />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 달력 본체: 현재달 & 다음달 듀얼 렌더링 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '32px' }}>
                        {renderCalendarMonth(viewDate.getFullYear(), viewDate.getMonth())}
                        {renderCalendarMonth(
                            viewDate.getMonth() === 11 ? viewDate.getFullYear() + 1 : viewDate.getFullYear(),
                            (viewDate.getMonth() + 1) % 12
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Bell, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationItem {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    message: string;
    type: string;
    createdAt: string;
}

interface NotificationBellProps {
    userRole: string;
    isLoggedIn: boolean;
}

export function NotificationBell({ userRole, isLoggedIn }: NotificationBellProps) {
    const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
    const [isNotiOpen, setIsNotiOpen] = useState(false);
    const notiRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 알림 목록 조회 (ROLE_HOST 및 ROLE_ADMIN 전용)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isLoggedIn && (userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' || userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
            fetch('/api/v1/notifications', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : []))
                .then((data: NotificationItem[]) => {
                    const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
                    const filtered = data.filter((item) => !dismissed.includes(item.id));
                    setNotifications(filtered);
                })
                .catch(() => setNotifications([]));
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn, userRole]);

    // 알림창 바깥 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutsideNoti = (event: MouseEvent) => {
            if (notiRef.current && !notiRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsNotiOpen(false);
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
    }, []);

    // X 버튼 클릭 시 로컬 삭제 처리
    const handleDeleteNoti = (id: string) => {
        const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
        if (!dismissed.includes(id)) {
            dismissed.push(id);
            localStorage.setItem('dismissedNotiIds', JSON.stringify(dismissed));
        }
        setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    };

    // 호스트나 관리자가 아니면 아무것도 렌더링하지 않음
    if (!isLoggedIn || (userRole !== 'ROLE_HOST' && userRole !== 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={notiRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsNotiOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    position: 'relative',
                    width: '38px',
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    border: '1.5px solid #123f4d',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    color: '#123f4d'
                }}
                aria-label="알림"
            >
                <Bell size={18} />

                {notifications.length > 0 && (
                    <span
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '3px',
                            left: '3px',
                            width: '8px',
                            height: '8px',
                            backgroundColor: '#e53e3e',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: '1.5px solid #ffffff'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </button>

            {isNotiOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '115%',
                        right: 0,
                        width: '300px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '12px',
                        zIndex: 1100
                    }}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            marginBottom: '8px',
                            paddingBottom: '6px',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9'
                        }}
                    >
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b' }}>알림 내역</strong>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b' }}>{notifications.length}건</span>
                    </div>

                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ padding: '20px 0', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                            새로운 알림이 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', maxHeight: '250px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                            {notifications.map((item) => (
                                <div
                                    key={item.id}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                        padding: '8px 10px',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        gap: '8px'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div>
                                        <p
                                            style={{
                                                margin: '0 0 3px 0',
                                                fontSize: '12px',
                                                fontWeight: 600,
                                                color: item.type === 'ACCOM_REJECT' ? '#e53e3e' : '#0f766e'
                                            }}
                                        >
                                            {item.title}
                                        </p>
                                        <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: '#475569', wordBreak: 'break-word', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
                                            {item.message}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => handleDeleteNoti(item.id)}
                                        style={{
                                            background: 'none',
                                            border: 'none',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            padding: '2px',
                                            color: '#94a3b8'
                                        }}
                                        aria-label="알림 지우기"
                                    >
                                        <X size={14} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/HostApplyBanner.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { requestApplyHost } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import { X, Building } from 'lucide-react';

export function HostApplyBanner() {
    const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token) {
            try {
                const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
                const decodedPayload = JSON.parse(
                    decodeURIComponent(
                        atob(base64Payload)
                            .split('')
                            .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                            .join('')
                    )
                );
                // 토큰에서 역할 추출
                setUserRole(decodedPayload.role || null);
            } catch {
                setUserRole(null);
            }
        } else {
            setUserRole(null);
        }
    }, []);

    // 💡 로그인하지 않았거나, 일반 사용자(ROLE_USER)가 아니면 화면에 아예 그리지 않음
    if (userRole !== 'ROLE_USER') {
        return null;
    }

    const handleApply = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('사업자등록번호를 입력해주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        setErrorMessage('');

        try {
            const data = await requestApplyHost(businessNo.trim());
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('숙소 관리자 신청이 완료되었습니다! 파트너 권한으로 전환됩니다.');
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '신청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (

        // 숙소 관리자 신청
        <section style={{ backgroundColor: '#0f333f', padding: '24px 0', borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)' }}>
            <div className="container" style={{
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: '1fr auto 1fr', // 💡 좌(비움) : 중앙(문구) : 우(버튼) 3분할
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '16px'
            }}>
                {/* 1. 좌측 균형을 맞추기 위한 빈 영역 */}
                <div />

                {/* 2. 중앙으로 배치되는 문구 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.15)',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center'
                    }}>
                        <Building size={20} color="#6ee7b7" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <strong style={{ display: 'block', color: '#ffffff', fontSize: '15px' }}>
                            숙소를 신청하고 싶은가요?
                        </strong>
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                파트너로 등록하고 더 많은 여행객에게 숙소를 소개해 보세요.
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 우측 끝에 고정되는 버튼 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
                        style={{
                            padding: '10px 22px',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            backgroundColor: '#6ee7b7',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '24px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.2)',
                        }}
                    >
                        숙소관리자 신청
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 사업자번호 입력 모달창 */}
            {isModalOpen && (
                <div style={{
                    position: 'fixed',
                    top: 0,
                    left: 0,
                    width: '100vw',
                    height: '100vh',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    zIndex: 2000
                }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '90%',
                        maxWidth: '400px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        padding: '28px',
                        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)',
                        position: 'relative'
                    }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                            style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '18px', right: '18px', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        >
                            <X size={20} color="#64748b" />
                        </button>

                        <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px 0', fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a' }}>숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청</h3>
                        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 20px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                            운영 중이신 사업자등록번호를 입력하시면 즉시 호스트 권한이 부여됩니다.
                        </p>

                        {errorMessage && (
                            <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
                                {errorMessage}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <form onSubmit={handleApply}>
                            <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    사업자등록번호
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={businessNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        padding: '10px 12px',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    padding: '11px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '신청 진행 중...' : '신청 완료'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/TourSliderSection.tsx
