import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    Heart,
    Navigation,
    MapPin
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchMyTourWishlist, toggleTourLike } from '../api/tours';

export interface TourSliderItem {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    firstImage?: string;
    addr1?: string;
    distanceKm?: number; // 💡 숙소 상세페이지에서는 전달되고, 홈페이지에서는 생략됨
}

interface TourSliderSectionProps {
    tours: TourSliderItem[];
    headerRender?: React.ReactNode; // 상단 좌측 제목 영역 커스텀
    footerRender?: React.ReactNode; // 하단 '더 많은 관광지를 둘러보세요' 버튼 등 커스텀
    containerStyle?: React.CSSProperties;
}

export function TourSliderSection({
                                      tours,
                                      headerRender,
                                      footerRender,
                                      containerStyle
                                  }: TourSliderSectionProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [slideIndex, setSlideIndex] = useState(0);
    const [likedTourIds, setLikedTourIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    const VISIBLE_CARDS = 3;
    const CARD_GAP = 16;
    const displayTours = tours.slice(0, 9); // 최대 9개 노출
    const maxSlideIndex = Math.max(0, displayTours.length - VISIBLE_CARDS);

    const currentPage = Math.floor(slideIndex / VISIBLE_CARDS) + 1;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(displayTours.length / VISIBLE_CARDS));

    // 💡 전달받은 관광지 목록(tours)이 바뀌면 슬라이더를 첫 페이지(0)로 리셋
    useEffect(() => {
        setSlideIndex(0);
    }, [tours]);

    // 로그인한 유저의 관광지 찜 목록 동기화
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            setLikedTourIds(new Set());
            return;
        }
        fetchMyTourWishlist()
            .then((list) => {
                setLikedTourIds(new Set(list.map((t) => t.contentId)));
            })
            .catch(() => setLikedTourIds(new Set()));
    }, []);

    // 찜(하트) 토글 핸들러
    const handleToggleLike = async (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => {
        e.stopPropagation();

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 기능입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const res = await toggleTourLike(contentId);
            setLikedTourIds((prev) => {
                const next = new Set(prev);
                if (res.liked) {
                    next.add(contentId);
                } else {
                    next.delete(contentId);
                }
                return next;
            });
        } catch {
            alert('관광지 위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    if (displayTours.length === 0) return null;

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            {/* 상단 헤더 + 우측 3개 단위 페이지 화살표 컨트롤 */}
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'flex-end',
                marginBottom: '22px',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                gap: '12px'
            }}>
                <div>{headerRender}</div>

                {displayTours.length > VISIBLE_CARDS && (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 700, marginRight: '4px' }}>
                            {currentPage} / {totalPages} 페이지
                        </span>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={slideIndex === 0}
                            onClick={() => setSlideIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - VISIBLE_CARDS))}
                            aria-label="이전 3개 관광지 보기"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                borderRadius: '50%',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: slideIndex === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff',
                                color: slideIndex === 0 ? '#cbd5e1' : '#123f4d',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                cursor: slideIndex === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                                boxShadow: slideIndex === 0 ? 'none' : '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)'
                            }}
                        >
                            <ChevronLeft size={18} />
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex}
                            onClick={() => setSlideIndex((prev) => Math.min(maxSlideIndex, prev + VISIBLE_CARDS))}
                            aria-label="다음 3개 관광지 보기"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                borderRadius: '50%',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff',
                                color: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? '#cbd5e1' : '#123f4d',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                cursor: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                                boxShadow: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? 'none' : '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)'
                            }}
                        >
                            <ChevronRight size={18} />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 3개 노출 슬라이더 뷰포트 */}
            <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', width: '100%', padding: '4px 0' }}>
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        gap: `${CARD_GAP}px`,
                        transform: `translateX(calc(-${slideIndex} * ((100% + ${CARD_GAP}px) / ${VISIBLE_CARDS})))`,
                        transition: 'transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.25, 1, 0.5, 1)',
                    }}
                >
                    {displayTours.map((tour) => {
                        const isLiked = likedTourIds.has(tour.contentId);
                        const hasDistance = tour.distanceKm !== undefined && tour.distanceKm !== null;
                        const regionLabel = tour.addr1?.includes('서귀포') ? '서귀포시' : '제주시';

                        return (
                            <article
                                key={tour.contentId}
                                onClick={() => navigate(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`)}
                                style={{
                                    flex: `0 0 calc((100% - ${(VISIBLE_CARDS - 1) * CARD_GAP}px) / ${VISIBLE_CARDS})`,
                                    borderRadius: '16px',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    overflow: 'hidden',
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)',
                                    transition: 'transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease',
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-4px)';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 10px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.09)';
                                }}
                                onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 2px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)';
                                }}
                            >
                                {/* 상단 썸네일 이미지 + 좌측 상단(거리 또는 지역) 뱃지 + 우측 상단 찜 하트 */}
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        height: '185px',
                                        backgroundImage: `url(${tour.firstImage || '/jeju-stays.png'})`,
                                        backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                        backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                        position: 'relative',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <span
                                        style={{
                                            position: 'absolute',
                                            top: '12px',
                                            left: '12px',
                                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(18, 63, 77, 0.88)',
                                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                            color: '#ffffff',
                                            padding: '4px 10px',
                                            borderRadius: '20px',
                                            fontSize: '11px',
                                            fontWeight: 700,
                                            display: 'inline-flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            gap: '4px',
                                            boxShadow: '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)'
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        {hasDistance ? (
                                            <>
                                                <Navigation size={11} color="#5eead4" />
                                                숙소에서 {tour.distanceKm}km
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            <>
                                                <MapPin size={11} color="#5eead4" />
                                                {regionLabel}
                                            </>
                                        )}
                                    </span>

                                    {/* 우측 상단 관광지 찜(하트) 버튼 */}
                                    <div
                                        onClick={(e) => handleToggleLike(e, tour.contentId)}
                                        role="button"
                                        aria-label="관광지 위시리스트 토글"
                                        style={{
                                            position: 'absolute',
                                            top: '12px',
                                            right: '12px',
                                            width: '34px',
                                            height: '34px',
                                            borderRadius: '50%',
                                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
                                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                            display: 'flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            justifyContent: 'center',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                                            zIndex: 2,
                                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                                        }}
                                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.12)')}
                                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                                    >
                                        <Heart
                                            size={18}

                                        overflow: 'hidden',

                                    </h3>
                                    <div style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        paddingTop: '10px',
                                        borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        color: '#64748b',
                                        fontWeight: 600
                                    }}>
                                        <span>관광지 상세정보 보기</span>
                                        <ChevronRight size={15} color="#0f766e" />
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </article>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 하단 추가 버튼 영역 (홈페이지의 '더 많은 관광지를 둘러보세요' 버튼 등) */}
            {footerRender && (
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '32px' }}>
                    {footerRender}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Heart, MapPin, ArrowRight, BedDouble } from 'lucide-react';
import { getLodgingTypeName, money } from '../data/stays';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

interface StayCardProps {
    stay: Stay;
    isLiked: boolean;
    isRemoving?: boolean;
    showOverview?: boolean;
    onCardClick: (stay: Stay) => void;
    onToggleLike: (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => void;
}

export function StayCard({
                             stay,
                             isLiked,
                             isRemoving = false,
                             showOverview = true,
                             onCardClick,
                             onToggleLike,
                         }: StayCardProps) {
    const remaining = stay.remainingRooms ?? 0;
    const isUrgent = remaining > 0 && remaining <= 5;

    return (
        <article
            className="stay-card"
            onClick={() => onCardClick(stay)}
            style={{
                cursor: 'pointer',
                position: 'relative',
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                opacity: isRemoving ? 0 : 1,
                transform: isRemoving ? 'scale(0.92)' : 'scale(1)',
                pointerEvents: isRemoving ? 'none' : 'auto',
                transition: 'opacity 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1), transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
            }}
        >
            <div>
                {/* 상단 숙소 이미지 및 태그/하트 버튼 */}
                <div
                    className="stay-photo"
                    style={{
                        backgroundImage: `url(${stay.firstImage || stay.firstImage2 || '/jeju-stays.png'})`,
                        backgroundSize: 'cover',
                        backgroundPosition: 'center',
                        backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <span className="photo-tag">
                        {getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </span>

                    <div
                        onClick={(e) => onToggleLike(e, stay.contentId)}
                        role="button"
                        aria-label="위시리스트 토글"
                        style={{

                            top: '12px',
                            right: '12px',
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88)',
                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                            zIndex: 2,
                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.1)')}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                    >
                        <Heart
                            size={19}
                            fill={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : 'none'}
                            color={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : '#64748b'}
                            strokeWidth={isLiked ? 2 : 1.8}
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 하단 숙소 정보 영역 */}
                <div className="stay-info">
                    {/* 지역명(좌측) + 잔여 객실 뱃지(우측) */}
                    <div
                        className="stay-location"
                        style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}
                    >
                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px' }}>
                            <MapPin size={15} />
                            {stay.addr?.includes('서귀포') ? '서귀포시' : '제주시'}
                        </span>

                        {/* 💡 잔여 객실 수 뱃지 */}
                        {stay.remainingRooms !== undefined && (
                            <span
                                style={{
                                    display: 'inline-flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '4px',
                                    padding: '2px 8px',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    backgroundColor:
                                        remaining === 0
                                            ? '#f1f5f9'
                                            : isUrgent
                                                ? '#fef2f2'
                                                : '#f0fdfa',
                                    color:
                                        remaining === 0
                                            ? '#94a3b8'
                                            : isUrgent
                                                ? '#dc2626'
                                                : '#0f766e',
                                    border: `1px solid ${
                                        remaining === 0
                                            ? '#e2e8f0'
                                            : isUrgent
                                                ? '#fecaca'
                                                : '#ccfbf1'
                                    }`,
                                }}
                            >
                                <BedDouble size={12} />
                                {remaining === 0
                                    ? '예약 마감'
                                    : isUrgent
                                        ? `마감임박 · 잔여 ${remaining}실`
                                        : `잔여 ${remaining}실`}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <h3>{stay.title}</h3>
                    {showOverview && (
                        <p>
                            {stay.overview
                                ? `${stay.overview.slice(0, 80)}...`
                                : '편안한 휴식을 제공하는 제주 숙소입니다.'}
                        </p>
                    )}

                    <div className="stay-bottom">
                        <div>
                            <strong>{money(stay.basePrice)}</strong>
                            <small> / 1박 기준가</small>
                        </div>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                onCardClick(stay);
                            }}
                            aria-label={`${stay.title} 예약 정보 보기`}
                        >
                            <ArrowRight size={19} />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { X, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import type { TourSummary } from '../api/tours';
import { KakaoTourMap } from './KakaoTourMap';

interface TourMapModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    tours: TourSummary[];
}

export function TourMapModal({ isOpen, onClose, tours }: TourMapModalProps) {
    const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
            setKeyword('');
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    const filteredTours = useMemo(() => {
        if (!keyword.trim()) return tours;
        const kw = keyword.trim().toLowerCase();
        return tours.filter(
            (t) =>
                t.title?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                t.addr1?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                t.addr2?.toLowerCase().includes(kw)
        );
    }, [tours, keyword]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px',
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1100px',
                    height: '85vh',
                    maxHeight: '780px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    padding: '28px',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    aria-label="닫기"
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '24px',
                        right: '24px',
                        background: '#f1f5f9',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        zIndex: 10,
                    }}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* 상단 헤더 및 검색바 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-end',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '12px',
                        marginBottom: '18px',
                        paddingRight: '48px',
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <span
                            style={{
                                color: '#0f766e',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 800,
                                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                                display: 'block',
                                marginBottom: '4px',
                            }}
                        >
                            JEJU TOUR ATTRACTION MAP
                        </span>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            제주 관광 지도
                        </h2>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            지도 위의 마커를 클릭하면 관광지 사진과 정보를 확인하고 상세페이지로 이동할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={keyword}
                                onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="관광지명 또는 지역 검색"
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 14px 8px 34px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    outline: 'none',
                                    width: '210px',
                                }}
                            />
                            <Search size={15} color="#64748b" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '12px', top: '10px' }} />
                        </div>

                        <span
                            style={{
                                color: '#0f766e',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                                padding: '7px 14px',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                            }}
                        >
                            관광명소 {filteredTours.length}곳 표시 중
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 지도 영역 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        minHeight: 0,
                        borderRadius: '18px',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <KakaoTourMap
                        tours={filteredTours}
                        level={9}
                        height="100%"
                        mode="search"
                        onNavigateDetail={onClose}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/WeatherWidget.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
    Cloud,
    Sun,
    CloudSun,
    CloudRain,
    CloudSnow,
    CloudLightning,
    CloudDrizzle,
    CloudFog
} from 'lucide-react';

interface WeatherData {
    temp: number;
    description: string;
    main: string;
    iconCode: string;
}

export function WeatherWidget() {
    const [weather, setWeather] = useState<WeatherData | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const fetchWeather = async () => {
            try {
                let apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_OPENWEATHER_API_KEY || '';

                if (!apiKey) {
                    const keyRes = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/weather-key');
                    if (keyRes.ok) {
                        const keyData = await keyRes.json();
                        apiKey = keyData.apiKey;
                    }
                }

                if (!apiKey) {
                    if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
                    return;
                }

                // 💡 city: Jeju, units: metric (섭씨), lang: kr (한글 설명)
                const weatherRes = await fetch(
                    `https://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather?q=Jeju&appid=${apiKey}&units=metric&lang=kr`
                );

                if (!weatherRes.ok) {
                    throw new Error('날씨 API 호출 실패');
                }

                const data = await weatherRes.json();

                if (isMounted) {
                    setWeather({
                        temp: Math.round(data.main.temp),
                        description: data.weather[0]?.description || '',
                        main: data.weather[0]?.main || 'Clouds', // 날씨 대분류
                        iconCode: data.weather[0]?.icon || '01d',
                    });
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('날씨 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다:', err);
            } finally {
                if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
            }
        };

        fetchWeather();

        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    if (loading || !weather) {
        return null;
    }

    // 💡 첨부 화면의 디자인처럼 부드러운 벡터 아이콘으로 매핑
    const renderWeatherIcon = () => {
        const main = weather.main.toLowerCase();
        const iconCode = weather.iconCode;
        const iconSize = 19; // 1.5배 보기 좋은 크기

        // 1. 맑음
        if (main.includes('clear')) {
            return <Sun size={iconSize} color="#f59e0b" fill="#fef3c7" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 2. 구름
        if (main.includes('cloud')) {
            // 구름 조금 (02d, 02n)
            if (iconCode.startsWith('02')) {
                return <CloudSun size={iconSize} color="#475569" fill="#e2e8f0" strokeWidth={2} />;
            }
            // 구름 많음 / 흐림 (03d, 04d 등 - 첨부해주신 Today의 구름 모양!)
            return <Cloud size={iconSize} color="#64748b" fill="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 3. 비
        if (main.includes('rain')) {
            return <CloudRain size={iconSize} color="#0284c7" fill="#e0f2fe" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 4. 이슬비
        if (main.includes('drizzle')) {
            return <CloudDrizzle size={iconSize} color="#38bdf8" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 5. 눈
        if (main.includes('snow')) {
            return <CloudSnow size={iconSize} color="#67e8f9" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 6. 뇌우
        if (main.includes('thunderstorm')) {
            return <CloudLightning size={iconSize} color="#eab308" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        // 7. 안개 등
        return <CloudFog size={iconSize} color="#94a3b8" strokeWidth={2} />;
    };

    return (
        <div
            title={`제주도 현재 날씨: ${weather.description}`}
            style={{
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '6px',
                padding: '4px 12px',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(18, 63, 77, 0.05)',
                borderRadius: '24px',
                border: '1px solid #d5e0df',
                fontSize: '13px',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: '#17343d',
                userSelect: 'none',
                cursor: 'default',
                height: '34px',
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
            }}
        >
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 800 }}>제주</span>

            {/* 💡 구름/날씨 아이콘 */}
            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                {renderWeatherIcon()}
            </span>

            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', letterSpacing: '-0.3px' }}>{weather.temp}°C</span>
        </div>
    );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
//해당 컴포넌트는 예제용으로 차후 삭제 요망
interface ApiResponse {
    status: string;
    message: string;
    port: number;
}

export default function ApiTest() {
    const [data, setData] = useState<ApiResponse | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('/api/hello')
            .then((res) => {
                if (!res.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`HTTP 에러 발생: ${res.status}`);
                }
                return res.json();
            })
            .then((json: ApiResponse) => {
                setData(json);
                setLoading(false);
            })
            .catch((err) => {
                setError(err.message);
                setLoading(false);
            });
    }, []);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                margin: '20px auto',
                maxWidth: '600px',
                padding: '20px',
                border: '1px solid #4caf50',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f8e9',
                fontFamily: 'sans-serif',
            }}
        >
            <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', color: '#2e7d32' }}>
                🔗 Spring Boot & React 연동 상태
            </h3>

            {loading && <p>데이터를 불러오는 중입니다...</p>}
            {error && <p style={{ color: '#d32f2f' }}>연동 오류: {error}</p>}

            {data && (
                <div style={{ lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>상태:</strong> {data.status}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>서버 메시지:</strong> {data.message}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>응답 포트:</strong> {data.port}</p>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useReservation.ts

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { createReservation } from '../api/stays';
import type { Booking, Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useReservation(apiOnline: boolean) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Stay | null>(null);
  const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState('');
  const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState('');
  const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);
  const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = selected ? 'hidden' : '';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [selected]);

  const open = (stay: Stay) => { setSelected(stay); setFeedback(''); };
  const close = () => { setSelected(null); setFeedback(''); };

  const book = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!selected) return;
    if (checkOut <= checkIn) {
      setFeedback('퇴실일은 입실일 다음 날부터 선택해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (!apiOnline) {
      setFeedback('현재 예약 접수를 준비 중입니다.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setFeedback('');
    try {
      const booking: Booking = {
        contentId: selected.contentId, // 수정: contentId 바인딩
        checkIn,
        checkOut,
        guests,
        guestName,
        phone
      };
      const data = await createReservation(booking);
      setFeedback(`예약 요청이 접수되었습니다. 예약번호: ${data.reservationCode || data.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return { selected, open, close, checkIn, setCheckIn, checkOut, setCheckOut, guests, setGuests,
    guestName, setGuestName, phone, setPhone, submitting, feedback, book };
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const slideCount = 3;

export function useHeroSlider() {
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount), 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const previous = () => setSlide(current => (current + slideCount - 1) % slideCount);
  const next = () => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount);
  return { slide, previous, next };
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useStays.ts
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchStays } from '../data/stays';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useStays() {
  const [region, setRegion] = useState('전체 지역');
  const [type, setType] = useState('전체 숙박');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [appliedCheckIn, setAppliedCheckIn] = useState<string>('');
  const [appliedCheckOut, setAppliedCheckOut] = useState<string>('');
  const [appliedGuests, setAppliedGuests] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined); // 💡 추가

  const [stays, setStays] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
  const [apiOnline, setApiOnline] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);

    fetchStays(region, type, term, appliedCheckIn, appliedCheckOut, appliedGuests) // 💡 전달
        .then(data => {
          if (active) {
            setStays(data);
            setApiOnline(true);
          }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.error('숙소 로딩 실패:', err);
          if (active) setApiOnline(false);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (active) setLoading(false);
        });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [region, type, term, appliedCheckIn, appliedCheckOut, appliedGuests]);

  const selectRegion = (value: string) => {
    setRegion(value);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  };

  // 💡 inDate, outDate와 함께 guestsCount 수신
  const search = (inDate?: string, outDate?: string, guestsCount?: number) => {
    setTerm(query);
    if (inDate !== undefined) setAppliedCheckIn(inDate);
    if (outDate !== undefined) setAppliedCheckOut(outDate);
    if (guestsCount !== undefined) setAppliedGuests(guestsCount);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return {
    region,
    setRegion,
    type,
    setType,
    query,
    setQuery,
    result: stays,
    loading,
    apiOnline,
    selectRegion,
    search,
  };
}

import axios from 'axios';

export interface TourSummary {
    contentId: string;
    contentTypeId?: string;
    title: string;
    addr1?: string;
    addr2?: string;
    tel?: string;
    useTime?: string;
    restDate?: string;
    useFee?: string;
    firstImage?: string;
    mapX?: string;
    mapY?: string;
    overview?: string;
}

export interface TourImageItem {
    imageId: number;
    originImgUrl: string;
    imgName?: string;
}

export interface TourDetail extends TourSummary {
    zipcode?: string;
    openDate?: string;
    parking?: string;
    parkingFee?: string;
    images: TourImageItem[];
}

// 숙소 반경 20km 이내 주변 관광지 응답 타입 (NearbyTourResponseDto 매칭)
export interface NearbyTourItem {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    firstImage?: string;
    distanceKm: number;
}

export async function fetchTours(): Promise<TourSummary[]> {
    const res = await axios.get<TourSummary[]>('/api/v1/tours');
    return res.data;
}

export async function fetchTourDetail(contentId: string): Promise<TourDetail> {
    const res = await axios.get<TourDetail>(`/api/v1/tours/${contentId}`);
    return res.data;
}

export async function toggleTourLike(contentId: string): Promise<{ liked: boolean; message: string }> {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const res = await axios.post<{ liked: boolean; message: string }>(
        `/api/v1/tours/likes/${contentId}`,
        {},
        { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
    );
    return res.data;
}

export async function fetchMyTourWishlist(): Promise<TourSummary[]> {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const res = await axios.get<TourSummary[]>('/api/v1/tours/likes/my', {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
    });
    return res.data;
}

// 숙소 상세페이지 주변 20km 이내 관광지 조회 (최대 9개)
export async function fetchNearbyTours(accomContentId: string): Promise<NearbyTourItem[]> {
    const res = await axios.get<NearbyTourItem[]>('/api/v1/tours/nearby', {
        params: { accomId: accomContentId }
    });
    return res.data;
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/api/stays.ts

import type { Booking, BookingResponse, Stay } from '../types/stay';

const apiBase = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '').replace(/\/$/, '');

export async function fetchStays(): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/stays`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  const stays: unknown = await response.json();
  if (!Array.isArray(stays)) throw new Error('숙소 응답 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  return stays as Stay[];
}

export async function createReservation(booking: Booking): Promise<BookingResponse> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/reservations`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(booking),
  });
  const data: BookingResponse = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || '예약 요청을 처리하지 못했습니다.');
  return data;
}

export async function toggleAccommodationLike(contentId: string): Promise<{ liked: boolean; message: string }> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/likes/accommodations/${contentId}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
  return response.json();
}

// 💡 내 위시리스트 숙소 목록 조회
export async function fetchMyWishlist(checkIn?: string, checkOut?: string, guests?: number): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (checkIn && checkIn.trim()) params.append('checkIn', checkIn.trim());
  if (checkOut && checkOut.trim()) params.append('checkOut', checkOut.trim());
  if (guests && guests > 0) params.append('guests', String(guests));

  const queryString = params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : '';
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/likes/accommodations/my${queryString}`, {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('위시리스트를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  return response.json();
}

import axios from 'axios';

export interface SignupPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    name: string;
    businessNo?: string | null;
}

export interface LoginPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
}

export interface TokenResponse {
    token: string;
}

// 회원가입
export const requestSignup = async (payload: SignupPayload): Promise<string> => {
    const response = await axios.post<string>('/api/v1/auth/signup', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 일반 로그인
export const requestLogin = async (payload: LoginPayload): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 카카오 로그인 인증창 URL
const KAKAO_CLIENT_ID = '9c6e519edc47e02f6460edd2afd70d35';
const KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI = 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/kakao/callback';

export const KAKAO_AUTH_URL = `https://kauth.kakao.com/oauth/authorize?client_id=${KAKAO_CLIENT_ID}&redirect_uri=${KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI}&response_type=code&prompt=login`;
// 숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청 API
export const requestApplyHost = async (businessNo: string): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>(
        '/api/v1/auth/apply-host',
        {businessNo},
        {
            headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
        }
    );
    return response.data;
};

import axios from 'axios';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

// 1. 지역 필터
export const regions = [
 '전체 지역',
 '제주시',
 '애월·한림',
 '함덕·구좌',
 '서귀포·중문',
 '성산·표선',
] as const;

// 2. 숙박 유형 필터
export const types = [
 '전체 숙박',
 '호텔',
 '콘도/리조트',
 '펜션/풀빌라',
 '모텔',
 '캠핑/글램핑',
 '게스트하우스/호스텔',
] as const;

// 3. 메인 추천 여행지 배너
export const destinations = [
 { name: '애월·한림', note: '해안도로와 석양', className: 'spot-a' },
 { name: '서귀포·중문', note: '폭포와 남쪽 바다', className: 'spot-b' },
 { name: '성산·표선', note: '일출과 오름', className: 'spot-c' },
];

// 4. 금액 포맷팅 헬퍼
export const money = (n?: number) => {
 if (!n || n <= 0) return '요금 문의';
 return new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR').format(n) + '원';
};

// 5. DB 코드(AC01~AC06)를 한글 숙박 유형명으로 변환
export const getLodgingTypeName = (code?: string): string => {
 switch (code) {
  case 'AC01': return '호텔';
  case 'AC02': return '콘도미니엄';
  case 'AC03': return '펜션/민박';
  case 'AC04': return '모텔';
  case 'AC05': return '캠핑';
  case 'AC06': return '호스텔';
  default: return code || '숙소';
 }
};

// 6. 지역 필터명을 백엔드 검색 키워드로 매핑
export const mapRegionToKeyword = (region: string): string => {
 if (region === '전체 지역' || !region) return '';
 if (region === '애월·한림') return '애월';
 if (region === '함덕·구좌') return '구좌';
 if (region === '서귀포·중문') return '서귀포';
 if (region === '성산·표선') return '성산';
 return region; // '제주시' 등
};

// 7. 숙박 유형명을 백엔드 파라미터(AC01~AC06)로 매핑
export const mapTypeToKeyword = (type: string): string => {
 if (!type || type === '전체 숙박') return '';
 if (type === '호텔') return 'AC01';
 if (type.includes('콘도') || type.includes('리조트')) return 'AC02';
 if (type.includes('펜션')) return 'AC03';
 if (type.includes('모텔')) return 'AC04';
 if (type.includes('캠핑')) return 'AC05';
 if (type.includes('호스텔') || type.includes('게스트하우스')) return 'AC06';
 return type;
};

// 8. 백엔드 숙소 목록 비동기 조회 함수
export const fetchStays = async (
    region?: string,
    type?: string,
    query?: string,
    checkIn?: string,
    checkOut?: string,
    guests?: number // 💡 추가
): Promise<Stay[]> => {
 const params: Record<string, string | number> = {};

 const regionKeyword = mapRegionToKeyword(region || '');
 if (regionKeyword) params.region = regionKeyword;

 const typeKeyword = mapTypeToKeyword(type || '');
 if (typeKeyword) params.type = typeKeyword;

 if (query && query.trim()) params.query = query.trim();
 if (checkIn && checkIn.trim()) params.checkIn = checkIn.trim();
 if (checkOut && checkOut.trim()) params.checkOut = checkOut.trim();
 if (guests && guests > 0) params.guests = guests; // 💡 추가

 const response = await axios.get<Stay[]>('/api/v1/stays', { params });
 return response.data;
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { requestLogin, KAKAO_AUTH_URL } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import {Footer} from "../components/Footer.tsx";

export function LoginPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const data = await requestLogin({ email, password });
            // JWT 토큰 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('로그인되었습니다!');
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '이메일 또는 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: '420px', margin: '60px auto', padding: '32px', border: '1px solid #e0e0e0', borderRadius: '12px', backgroundColor: '#fff' }}>
            <h2 style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '8px', color: '#123f4d' }}>로그인</h2>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#666', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>제주 야호 서비스를 이용해보세요.</p>

            {/* 카카오 로그인 버튼 */}
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    padding: '12px',
                    backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                    color: '#000000',
                    border: 'none',
                    borderRadius: '6px',
                    fontWeight: 600,
                    fontSize: '14px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    marginBottom: '20px'
                }}
            >
                카카오로 시작하기
            </button>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#aaa', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
                <span style={{ padding: '0 10px' }}>또는 이메일로 로그인</span>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
            </div>

            {errorMessage && (
                <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fde8e8', color: '#e02424', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                    {errorMessage}
                </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px' }}>
                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>이메일</label>
                    <input
                        type="email"
                        required
                        value={email}
                        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>비밀번호</label>
                    <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={password}
                        onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    style={{
                        marginTop: '10px',
                        padding: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                        color: '#fff',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    }}
                >
                    {loading ? '로그인 중...' : '로그인'}
                </button>
            </form>

            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '20px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#666' }}>
                아직 계정이 없으신가요? <Link to="/signup" style={{ color: '#123f4d', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none' }}>회원가입</Link>
            </div>
            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '10px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#666' }}>
                비밀번호를 잊으셨나요? <Link to="/find-password" style={{ color: '#123f4d', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none' }}>비밀번호 찾기</Link>
            </div>
        </div>
            <Footer/>
        </>
    );
}
